Per AGENT.md's stop-and-ask rule (a host reboot kills all four co-located agents' sessions/services — Tidal's was mid-flight), wrote the full findings + question ("reboot yourself / delegate a scheduled reboot / retire the anomaly") into `ASK.md` Open and flagged it to Josh via Telegram. **River did NOT reboot unilaterally.** All 11 services active throughout; alert is informational, host otherwise healthy (load 1.29 peak, disk fine).
